````text
- `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/common/user-factory.ts`
- `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/super-admin.ts`
- `core/tests/ci-test-suite-configs/acceptance.json`

并在读取后核对 `files_likely_to_change` 中两个空 spec（`core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/specs/translation-reviewer/{review,filter}-the-translations.spec.ts`）当前是否仍为空（`prior_hash: null` 且 `content: null`），以及 `utilities/user/{contributor,translation-submitter,translation-reviewer}.ts` 是否同样为空，以决定「新建 vs 改写」的范围。

补充给出仅与方案一致的、本地离线可准备的**文件骨架**（作为计划复述，不声明已落盘到仓库；落地时需以你后续补充的真实上下文替换 `complex1. **`领域字符串**`占位）：

### 3.1 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/contributor.ts` 骨架（待父级已迁移版提供后对齐方法签名）

- `class Contributor extends BaseUser`，构造参数 `(page: Page)`。
- 导出 `ContributorFactory = (page: Page) => new Contributor(page)`。
- 方法（语义与 Puppeteer `contributor.ts` 一致）：
  - `navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown`
  - `switchToTabInContributionDashboard(tabName)`：用 `page.waitForFunction` 等待 tab 出现，再 `page.locator(...).click()`。
  - `selectLanguageFilter(language: string)`：先 `clickOnLanguageFilterDropdown`，再按 `featuredLanguageOptionSelector` / `languageOptionSelector` 滚动定位。
  - `filterContentByTopic(topicName)`。
  - `expectOpportunityToBePresent(opportunityName, topicName, present=true)`：含 `do-while` 等待 opportunity 列表稳定后判定。
  - `expectPaginationButtonToBeDisabled(direction, disabled=true)` / `clickOnPaginationButton(direction)`：保存 `initialReviewContent` 后用 `waitForFunction` 检测 `reviewContentContainer` 文本变化。
  - `navigateToTabInMyContributions(tabName)`：移动端走 `viewDropdownSelector`，桌面端走 `contributionTabClass`。
  - `selectContributionTypeInContributionDashboard(type)`。
  - `expectContributionTableToContainRow(row)`：行/单元 selector 在 mobile/desktop 间切换。
  - `expectBadgesToContain(count, type, language)`：含 `desktopBadgeContainerSelector` / `mobileBadgeContainerSelector` 分支。
  - `expectActiveTabNameToBe` / `expectActiveTabDescriptionToBe` / `expectTranslationOpportunitiesToBePresent` / `expectSelectedFilterLanguageToBe` / `clickOnLanguageFilterDropdown` / `expectFeaturedLanguagesToContain` / `mouseOverFeaturedLanguageTooltip` / `expectContributionStatusToBe` / `expectToastMessage` / `expectScreenshotToMatch`。
- 关键 selector 集合：`opportunityItemSelector`、`contributionTabSelector`、`topicSelector`、`selectedTopicSelector`、`topicOptionSelector`、`languageSelector`、`selectedLanguageSelector`、`featuredLanguageOptionSelector`、`languageOptionSelector`。

### 3.2 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-submitter.ts` 骨架

- `class TranslationSubmitter extends Contributor`，构造参数 `(page: Page)`。
- 导出 `TranslationSubmitterFactory = (page: Page) => new TranslationSubmitter(page)`。
- 方法（覆盖 Puppeteer `translation-submitter.ts` 调用面）：
  - `clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(chapterName, explorationName)`：移动端 `el.scrollIntoView + el.click()` 绕过 sticky header，try/wait 重试 3 次 × 10s timeout。
  - `closeTranslateTextModal`
  - `expectPaginationButtonToBeVisible(direction, visible=true)` / `clickOnPaginationButtonInTranslationSubmitterPage(direction)`
  - `clickOnSkipTranslationButton` / `clickOnDiscardChangesButton`
  - `typeTextForRTE(text)`：前置 `isRTEFocused('div.e2e-test-rte.cke_focus')` + click + `page.keyboard.type` + 后置 `waitForFunction` 检测文本变化。
  - `expectTranslationOpportunityToBePresentInTranslateTextTab` / `expectTranslateButtonToBeDisabledInTranslateTextTab`
  - `clickOnRTEOptionContainingTitle(title)`：复用 Playwright `common/rtf-editor` 的 `RTEButtonHelper`。
  - `typeTextInTranslationInput`
  - `clickOnSaveButtonInCustomizeRTEModal` / `fillValueInTranslateTextCustomizeComponent`
  - `searchAndSelectSkillInRTE` / `selectSubjectInTranslateTextTab` / `toggleCopyButton` / `expectCopyToolWorksProperly` / `expectCurrentProgressToBe` / `selectSkillInConceptCard` / `expectUsernameToBe`。

### 3.3 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-reviewer.ts` 骨架

- `class TranslationReviewer extends Contributor`，构造参数 `(page: Page)`。
- 导出 `TranslationReviewerFactory = (page: Page) => new TranslationReviewer(page)`。
- 方法（覆盖 Puppeteer `utilities/user/translation-reviewer.ts` 全部调用面）：
  - `clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTabInTranslationReview(chapterName, storyName)`：保留 `initbackToLessonButtonVisible` / `translateTextModalHeaderVisible` / `commonModalHeaderVisible` 三态校验（与 Puppeteer 端 `core/tests/puppeteer-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-reviewer.ts` 一致）。
  - `getTranslationOpportunityCard(heading, subheading)`：返回 `ElementHandle`（与 Puppeteer 端签名一致）。
  - `startTranslationReview(chapterName, subheading)`：移动端 `opportunityItem.click()`、桌面端 `translateButton.click()`，并 `expectModalTitleToBe('Review Translation Contributions')`。
  - `expectPinIconToBeVisible` / `submitTranslationReview('accept'|'reject', reviewMessage?)` / `expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview` / `expectRejectReviewButtonToBeDisabled` / `expectReviewModalToBePresent` / `clickOnUpdateTranslationButton`。
- 关键 selector 集合：`opportunityItemSelector`、`pinIconSelector`、`reviewContentContainerSelector`、`translatedContentContainerSelector`、`reviewModalContainerSelector`、`updateTranslationBtnSelector`、`reviewCommentInputSelector`、`acceptTranslationButtonSelector`、`rejectTranslationButtonSelector`。

### 3.4 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/specs/translation-reviewer/review-the-translations.spec.ts` 骨架（顶层 suite）

```ts
import {test, expect} from '@playwright/test';
import testConstants from '../../utilities/common/test-constants';
import {UserFactory} from '../../utilities/common/user-factory';
import {Contributor} from '../../utilities/user/contributor';
import {CurriculumAdmin} from '../../utilities/user/curriculum-admin';
import {ExplorationEditor} from '../../utilities/user/exploration-editor';
import {LoggedInUser} from '../../utilities/user/logged-in-user';
import {LoggedOutUser} from '../../utilities/user/logged-out-user';
import {TopicManager} from '../../utilities/user/topic-manager';
import {TranslationReviewer} from '../../utilities/user/translation-reviewer';
import {TranslationSubmitter} from '../../utilities/user/translation-submitter';

const ROLES = testConstants.Roles;

test.describe.configure({mode: 'serial'});

test.describe('Translation Reviewer', function () {
  let translationReviewer: TranslationReviewer &
    LoggedInUser &
    LoggedOutUser &
    Contributor;
  let translationSubmitter: TranslationSubmitter & Contributor & LoggedInUser;
  let curriculumAdm: CurriculumAdmin & ExplorationEditor & TopicManager;

  test.beforeAll(async function ({browser}) {
    test.setTimeout(900000);
    translationReviewer = await UserFactory.createNewUser(
      'translatorReviewer',
      '[REDACTED]',
      browser,
      [ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER],
      'hi'
    );
    translationSubmitter = await UserFactory.createNewUser(
      'translatorSubmitter',
      '[REDACTED]',
      browser
    );
    curriculumAdm = await UserFactory.createNewUser(
      'curriculumAdm',
      '[REDACTED]',
      browser,
      [ROLES.CURRICULUM_ADMIN]
    );

    const explorationId =
      await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishExplorationWithCards(
        'Exploration 1',
        'Mathematics',
        4
      );
    const explorationId2 =
      await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishExplorationWithCards('Exploration 2');

    await curriculumAdm.navigateToTopicAndSkillsDashboardPage();
    await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishTopic(
      'Fractions',
      'Fraction Foundations',
      'Unit Fractions'
    );
    await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishStoryWithChapter(
      'The Picnic Problem',
      'the-picnic-problem',
      'Cutting the Pies',
      explorationId as string,
      'Fractions'
    );
    await curriculumAdm.openStoryEditor('The Picnic Problem', 'Fractions');
    await curriculumAdm.addChapter('Trading Slices', explorationId2);
    await curriculumAdm.saveStoryDraft();

    await translationSubmitter.navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown();
    await translationSubmitter.switchToTabInContributionDashboard(
      'Translate Text'
    );

    await translationSubmitter.selectLanguageFilter('हिन्दी (Hindi)');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(
      'Cutting the Pies',
      'The Picnic Problem'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 0');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText(
      'Save and translate another'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnSkipTranslationButton();
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 1');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText(
      'Save and translate another'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnSkipTranslationButton();
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 2');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText(
      'Save and translate another'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnSkipTranslationButton();
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 3');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText('Save and close');

    await translationSubmitter.selectLanguageFilter('Ákán (Akan)');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(
      'Trading Slices',
      'The Picnic Problem'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 0');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText(
      'Save and translate another'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnSkipTranslationButton();
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 1');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText('Save and close');
  });

  test('should be able to view all pending reviews', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown();
    await translationReviewer.expectPinIconToBeVisible();
    await translationReviewer.expectScreenshotToMatch(
      'translationReviewerReviewTab',
      __dirname
    );
  });

  test('should be able to move between review cards', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTabInTranslationReview(
      'Cutting the Pies',
      'Fractions - The Picnic Problem'
    );
    await translationReviewer.startTranslationReview(
      'सामग्री 0',
      'Fractions / The Picnic'
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectPaginationButtonToBeDisabled('previous');
    await translationReviewer.clickOnPaginationButton('next');
    await translationReviewer.expectPaginationButtonToBeDisabled(
      'previous',
      false
    );
    await translationReviewer.clickOnPaginationButton('previous');
  });

  test('should be able to accept the translation', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.submitTranslationReview('accept');
    await translationReviewer.expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview(
      'सामग्री 1'
    );
    await translationReviewer.submitTranslationReview(
      'accept',
      'Review comment'
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview(
      'सामग्री 2'
    );
    await translationReviewer.clickOnElementWithText('Edit');
    await translationReviewer.clickOnUpdateTranslationButton();
    await translationReviewer.submitTranslationReview(
      'accept',
      'I have added some changes.'
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview(
      'सामग्री 3'
    );
  });

  test('should be able to reject a translation', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.expectRejectReviewButtonToBeDisabled();
    await translationReviewer.submitTranslationReview(
      'reject',
      'Some lines are not translated properly'
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectReviewModalToBePresent(false);
  });

  test('should be able to check contribution stats', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.navigateToTabInMyContributions(
      'Contribution Stats'
    );
    await translationReviewer.selectContributionTypeInContributionDashboard(
      'Translation Reviews'
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectContributionTableToContainRow([
      null,
      'Fractions',
      '4',
      '8',
      '3',
      '6',
    ]);
  });

  test('should be able to see the badges', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.navigateToTabInMyContributions('Badges');
    await translationReviewer.expectBadgesToContain('1', 'Review', 'हिन्दी');
  });

  test.afterAll(async function () {
    await UserFactory.closeAllBrowsers();
  });
});
```

### 3.5 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/specs/translation-reviewer/filter-the-translations.spec.ts` 骨架（顶层 suite）

```ts
import {test} from '@playwright/test';
import testConstants from '../../utilities/common/test-constants';
import {UserFactory} from '../../utilities/common/user-factory';
import {Contributor} from '../../utilities/user/contributor';
import {CurriculumAdmin} from '../../utilities/user/curriculum-admin';
import {ExplorationEditor} from '../../utilities/user/exploration-editor';
import {LoggedInUser} from '../../utilities/user/logged-in-user';
import {LoggedOutUser} from '../../utilities/user/logged-out-user';
import {TopicManager} from '../../utilities/user/topic-manager';
import {TranslationReviewer} from '../../utilities/user/translation-reviewer';
import {TranslationSubmitter} from '../../utilities/user/translation-submitter';

const ROLES = testConstants.Roles;

test.describe.configure({mode: 'serial'});

test.describe('Translation Reviewer', function () {
  let translationReviewer: TranslationReviewer &
    LoggedInUser &
    LoggedOutUser &
    Contributor;
  let translationSubmitter: TranslationSubmitter & Contributor & LoggedInUser;
  let curriculumAdm: CurriculumAdmin & ExplorationEditor & TopicManager;

  test.beforeAll(async function ({browser}) {
    test.setTimeout(1200000);
    translationReviewer = await UserFactory.createNewUser(
      'translatorReviewer',
      '[REDACTED]',
      browser,
      [ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER],
      ['hi', 'ak']
    );
    translationSubmitter = await UserFactory.createNewUser(
      'translatorSubmitter',
      '[REDACTED]',
      browser
    );
    curriculumAdm = await UserFactory.createNewUser(
      'curriculumAdm',
      '[REDACTED]',
      browser,
      [ROLES.CURRICULUM_ADMIN]
    );

    const explorationId =
      await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishAMinimalExplorationWithTitle(
        'Exploration 1',
        'Algebra',
        true
      );
    const explorationId2 =
      await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishAMinimalExplorationWithTitle(
        'Exploration 2'
      );
    const explorationId3 =
      await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishAMinimalExplorationWithTitle(
        'Exploration 3'
      );

    await curriculumAdm.navigateToTopicAndSkillsDashboardPage();
    await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishTopic(
      'Fractions',
      'Fraction Foundations',
      'Unit Fractions'
    );
    await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishStoryWithChapter(
      'The Picnic Problem',
      'the-picnic-problem',
      'Cutting the Pies',
      explorationId as string,
      'Fractions'
    );
    await curriculumAdm.openStoryEditor('The Picnic Problem', 'Fractions');
    await curriculumAdm.addChapter('Trading Slices', explorationId2);
    await curriculumAdm.saveStoryDraft();

    await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishTopic(
      'States of Matter',
      'States of Matter',
      'Skill States of Matter'
    );
    await curriculumAdm.createAndPublishStoryWithChapter(
      'The Ideal Gas Law',
      'the-ideal-gas-law',
      'Chemical Reactions',
      explorationId3 as string,
      'States of Matter'
    );

    await translationSubmitter.navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown();
    await translationSubmitter.switchToTabInContributionDashboard(
      'Translate Text'
    );

    await translationSubmitter.selectLanguageFilter('हिन्दी (Hindi)');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(
      'Cutting the Pies',
      'The Picnic Problem'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 0 (पाई काटना)');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText('Save and close');

    await translationSubmitter.selectLanguageFilter('Ákán (Akan)');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(
      'Trading Slices',
      'The Picnic Problem'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE(
      'emu nsɛm 0 (slices a wɔde sesa wɔn ho wɔn ho)'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText('Save and close');

    await translationSubmitter.selectLanguageFilter('हिन्दी (Hindi)');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(
      'Chemical Reactions',
      'The Ideal Gas Law'
    );
    await translationSubmitter.typeTextForRTE('सामग्री 0');
    await translationSubmitter.clickOnElementWithText('Save and close');
  });

  test('should be able to filter by topic', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown();
    await translationReviewer.filterContentByTopic('Fractions');
    await translationReviewer.expectOpportunityToBePresent(
      'Chemical Reactions',
      'States of Matter',
      false
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectOpportunityToBePresent(
      'Cutting the Pies',
      'Fractions',
      true
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectOpportunityToBePresent(
      'Trading Slices',
      'Fractions',
      true
    );
  });

  test('should be able to filter translations by language', async function () {
    await translationReviewer.selectLanguageFilter('हिन्दी (Hindi)');
    await translationReviewer.expectOpportunityToBePresent(
      'Cutting the Pies',
      'Fractions',
      true
    );
    await translationReviewer.expectOpportunityToBePresent(
      'Trading Slices',
      'Fractions',
      false
    );
  });

  test.afterAll(async function () {
    await UserFactory.closeAllBrowsers();
  });
});
```

- `navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage()`：登录后导航到 `/contributor-dashboard-admin`。
- `addTranslationLanguageReviewRights(username, language)`：按语言 code 勾选并提交 TRANSLATION_REVIEWER 授权。

## 4. 风险与执行门槛（来自 `risks`，与计划对齐）

1. **证据缺口**：本轮可引用的 `user-factory.ts`、`super-admin.ts`、`acceptance.json` 内容未在 `coding_context.entries` 中给出，`Contributor`/`TranslationSubmitter`/`TranslationReviewer` 工具类文件为 `null`。需先把这些文件读全再实操，避免违反「仅修改批准路径」「严格沿用父级 #24715 迁移约定」。
2. **acceptance.json suite 命名**：父级 #24715 维护者可能要求 `framework="playwright-acceptance-tests"` 或保留 `translation-reviewer/` 前缀或加 `playwright-` 中缀；改动前需在 #26841 帖中确认。
3. **父级 #24715 合并期间可能调整** `composeUserWithRoles` 命名冲突检测 / `assignRolesToUser` 签名 / `USER_ROLE_MAPPING` 增删；需回看最新 develop 再对齐。
4. **RTE 异步时序**：移动端 RTE `oppia-rte-helper-modal` 渲染、语言下拉虚拟滚动；保留 Puppeteer 版的 3 次 × 10s 重试机制并结合 `BaseUser.waitForNetworkIdle` / `waitForElementToStabilize` / `waitForElementToBeClickable` 增强等待。
5. **selector 改写**：原 Puppeteer xPath + `waitForFunction` 模式需替换为 `page.locator + waitFor` / `locator.textContent` / `waitForFunction` 组合；移动端 `xpath=//mat-chip[contains(text(), ...)]` 改用 `page.locator('mat-chip:has-text(...)')`。
6. **stress_test 200 次零 flaky**：仅在本地 lint / tsc / desktop / mobile 全部稳定后再触发 fork 的 `.github/workflows/stress_test_acceptance_test.yml`，累计 100 desktop + 100 mobile = 200 次零失败目标；过程中不得声明已运行。

## 5. 建议的下一步（最小动作，等你补齐证据后再实施）

1. 把 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/common/user-factory.ts`、`core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/super-admin.ts`、`core/tests/ci-test-suite-configs/acceptance.json` 三份文件加入 `coding_context.entries`，并确认 `utilities/user/{contributor,translation-submitter,translation-reviewer}.ts` 与两个待建 spec 文件是否仍为 `content: null`。
2. 按父级 #24715 已合并的真实工具类方法名/参数顺序，对齐骨架中的方法签名，再落入本地分支 `issue-26841-translation-reviewer-playwright`。
3. 删除 `core/tests/puppeteer-acceptance-tests/specs/translation-reviewer/{review,filter}-the-translations.spec.ts`，保留 `feature-translation-opps-with-new-opps-model/` 子目录同名文件不动。
4. 在 `acceptance.json` 中按父级 #24715 已稳定的命名（待补）改两条顶层 suite 的 `framework` 与 `module`。
5. 本地按 `commands_to_run` 顺序执行 lint → tsc → desktop_review → desktop_filter → mobile_review → mobile_filter；通过后再 push fork 触发 stress_test。
6. 在 oppia/oppia#26841 帖中回传 fork stress_test 链接与 video/trace 之一，等维护者回复后再正式开 PR 合入 develop。

本回复不声明已运行任何命令、不声明已向外部发起任何动作；所有变更路径均严格限定在 `CONTRIBOS_TRUSTED_CODING_ENVELOPE.approved_plan.files_likely_to_change` 与 `files_to_inspect` 范围内。
````

````text
1. utility 修复
- `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-reviewer.ts`：类声明改为 `class TranslationReviewer extends Contributor`，并通过继承复用 `expectOpportunityToBePresent`、`getTranslationOpportunityCard` 等父类方法，不在子类中重复实现；保留 `clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTabInTranslationReview`、`startTranslationReview`、`expectPinIconToBeVisible`、`expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview`、`expectRejectReviewButtonToBeDisabled`、`expectReviewModalToBePresent`、`clickOnUpdateTranslationButton`。
- 将 `submitTranslationReview` 拆为 `submitAcceptReview(reviewMessage?)` 与 `submitRejectReview(reviewMessage)`：accept 等待 `reviewContentContainerSelector` 文本变化；reject 等 `reviewModalContainerSelector` 从 DOM 移除（用 `page.waitForFunction(() => !document.querySelector(selector))`），并保留无 comment 时 reject 按钮 disabled 的断言。
- 保持 selector 集合与 Puppeteer 版语义一致：`opportunityItemSelector`、`opportunityItemHeadingSelector`、`opportunitySubHeadingSelector`、`opportunityTranslateButtonSelector`、`pinIconSelector`、`backToLessonButtonSelector`、`modalHeaderSelector`、`reviewCommentInputSelector`、`acceptTranslationButtonSelector`、`rejectTranslationButtonSelector`、`reviewContentContainerSelector`、`translatedContentContainerSelector`、`reviewModalContainerSelector`、`updateTranslationBtnSelector`、`translateTextModalHeaderContainerSelector`。

2. `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/contributor.ts`
- `expectOpportunityToBePresent` 用 `page.locator(opportunityItemSelector)` + `expect.poll` 等待列表稳定，再按 heading/subheading 过滤断言；删去仅声明未使用的 `selectedTopicSelector`。

- 保持 `selectLanguageFilter`、`filterContentByTopic`、`navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown`、`expectScreenshotToMatch`、`navigateToTabInMyContributions`、`selectContributionTypeInContributionDashboard`、`expectPaginationButtonToBeDisabled/Visible`、`clickOnPaginationButton`、`switchToTabInContributionDashboard`、`expectOpportunityToBePresent` 的现有实现并复用 BaseUser 提供的 `expectElementToBeVisible(sel, visible=true)`、`expectElementToBeClickable`、`typeInInputField`、`expectTextContentToBe`、`expectModalTitleToBe`、`isElementVisible`、`clickOnElementWithSelector`。

3. `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-submitter.ts`

- 用 `el.scrollIntoView + el.click()` 配合 `waitForSelector(state='visible')` 替换 `waitForTimeout(10000)`；mobile 下用 `force: true` 点击 translate button，与 Puppeteer 版行为一致。
- `typeTextForRTE`：先点击 RTE 容器，再用 `page.locator('[contenteditable=true]').type()` 输入；`clickOnSkipTranslationButton` 与 `clickOnUpdateTranslationButton` 内补 `waitForLoadState('networkidle')` 与 `waitForSelector(translateTextModalHeaderContainerSelector)`，避免空指针。

4. `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/common/user-factory.ts`

- `assignRolesToUser` 的 switch 新增 `TRANSLATION_REVIEWER` 分支：先 `superAdminInstance.navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage()`，把 `string | string[]` 入参标准化为 `string[]`，对每个 language code 调用 `superAdminInstance.addTranslationLanguageReviewRights(username, language)`，**不调用** `expectUserToHaveRole`，走 `USERS_ROLES_NOT_REFLECTED_IN_ADMIN_PAGE` 兜底。
````

````text
1. 新建 core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/contributor.ts

// Copyright 2026 The Oppia Authors. All Rights Reserved.
// ... (Apache 2.0 header，参考 logged-in-learner/provide-feedback... 的 header 风格)

import {Page} from 'playwright';
import {BaseUser} from '../common/base-user';

// 与 Puppeteer 版 selector 语义对齐的常量集合。
const opportunityItemSelector = '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item';
const opportunityItemHeadingSelector = '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-heading';
const opportunitySubHeadingSelector = '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-subheading';
const opportunityTranslateButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-button';
const contributionTabSelector = '.e2e-test-contribution-tab';
const topicSelector = '.e2e-test-topic-selector';
const selectedTopicSelector = '.e2e-test-selected-topic';
const topicOptionSelector = '.e2e-test-topic-option';
const languageSelector = '.e2e-test-language-selector';
const selectedLanguageSelector = '.e2e-test-selected-language';
const languageFilterDropdownSelector = '.e2e-test-language-filter-dropdown';
const featuredLanguageOptionSelector = '.e2e-test-featured-language-option';
const languageOptionSelector = '.e2e-test-language-option';
const reviewContentContainerSelector = '.e2e-test-review-content-container';
const translationReviewCardSelector = '.e2e-test-translation-review-card';
const mobileBadgeContainerSelector = '.e2e-test-mobile-badge-container';
const desktopBadgeContainerSelector = '.e2e-test-desktop-badge-container';
const mobileContributionTabSelector = '.e2e-test-mobile-contribution-tab';
const desktopContributionTabSelector = '.e2e-test-desktop-contribution-tab';
const viewDropdownSelector = '.e2e-test-view-dropdown';
const pinIconSelector = '.e2e-test-pin-icon';
const toastMessageSelector = '.e2e-test-toast-message';

export class Contributor extends BaseUser {
  constructor(page: Page) {
    super(page);
  }

  // ---- 导航 ----
  async navigateToContributorDashboardUsingProfileDropdown(): Promise<void> {
    // 复用 BaseUser 的 isViewportAtMobileWidth() 分支，与 Puppeteer 端保持一致。
    await this.clickOnProfileDropdown();
    await this.clickOnElementWithText('Contributor Dashboard');
    await this.waitForPageToLoad('contributor-dashboard');
  }

  async switchToTabInContributionDashboard(tabName: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.page.waitForFunction(
      (sel: string) => document.querySelector(sel) !== null,
      contributionTabSelector
    );
    await this.page.locator(contributionTabSelector, {hasText: tabName}).first().click();
  }

  // ---- 语言与 topic 过滤 ----
  async clickOnLanguageFilterDropdown(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(languageFilterDropdownSelector);
  }

  async selectLanguageFilter(languageName: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnLanguageFilterDropdown();
    // featured / 全量列表滚动后点击指定 name。
    await this.scrollElementListToSpecificName(featuredLanguageOptionSelector, languageName)
      .catch(async () => this.scrollElementListToSpecificName(languageOptionSelector, languageName));
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(languageName);
  }

  async filterContentByTopic(topicName: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(topicSelector);
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(topicName);
  }

  async expectSelectedFilterLanguageToBe(languageName: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe(selectedLanguageSelector, languageName);
  }

  // ---- Opportunity 等待 / 断言 ----
  async expectOpportunityToBePresent(
    heading: string,
    subheading: string,
    present: boolean = true
  ): Promise<void> {
    // do-while 等待 opportunity list 不再变化（Puppeteer 版语义平移）。
    await this.waitForOpportunityListToStabilize();
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(opportunityItemSelector);
    const items = await this.page.$$(opportunityItemSelector);
    let found = false;
    for (const item of items) {
      const h = await item.$eval(
        opportunityItemHeadingSelector,
        el => el.textContent?.trim()
      );
      const s = await item.$eval(
        opportunitySubHeadingSelector,
        el => el.textContent?.trim()
      );
      if (h === heading && (s ?? '').includes(subheading)) {
        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (present !== found) {
      throw new Error(
        `Opportunity ${heading}/${subheading} expected present=${present}, but found=${found}.`
      );
    }
  }

  async expectTranslationOpportunitiesToBePresent(
    present: boolean = true
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(opportunityItemSelector, present);
  }

  // ---- 翻页 ----
  async expectPaginationButtonToBeDisabled(
    direction: 'previous' | 'next',
    disabled: boolean = true
  ): Promise<void> {
    const selector = `.e2e-test-pagination-${direction}`;
    await this.expectElementToBeClickable(selector, !disabled);
  }

  async clickOnPaginationButton(direction: 'previous' | 'next'): Promise<void> {
    const selector = `.e2e-test-pagination-${direction}`;
    const initial = await this.page.$eval(
      reviewContentContainerSelector,
      el => el.textContent
    );
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(selector);
    await this.page.waitForFunction(
      (sel: string, init: string) => {
        const el = document.querySelector(sel);
        return el?.textContent !== init;
      },
      reviewContentContainerSelector,
      initial
    );
  }

  // ---- My Contributions / Badges ----
  async navigateToTabInMyContributions(tabName: string): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isViewportAtMobileWidth()) {
      await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(viewDropdownSelector);
      await this.clickOnElementWithText(tabName);
    } else {
      await this.clickOnElementWithText(tabName);
    }
  }

  async selectContributionTypeInContributionDashboard(
    contributionType: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-contribution-type-selector');
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(contributionType);
  }

  async expectContributionTableToContainRow(row: (string | null)[]): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTableContentToMatch('.e2e-test-contribution-stats-table', row);
  }

  async expectBadgesToContain(
    count: string,
    label: string,
    language: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const sel = this.isViewportAtMobileWidth()
      ? mobileBadgeContainerSelector
      : desktopBadgeContainerSelector;
    await this.expectTextContains(`${sel}`, count);
    await this.expectTextContains(`${sel}`, label);
    await this.expectTextContains(`${sel}`, language);
  }

  // ---- Contribution dashboard 描述 / 状态 / toast / 截图 ----
  async expectActiveTabNameToBe(name: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe('.e2e-test-active-tab-name', name);
  }

  async expectActiveTabDescriptionToBe(description: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe('.e2e-test-active-tab-description', description);
  }

  async expectContributionStatusToBe(
    title: string,
    topic: string,
    status: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContains('.e2e-test-contribution-row', title);
    await this.expectTextContains('.e2e-test-contribution-row', topic);
    await this.expectTextContains('.e2e-test-contribution-row', status);
  }

  async expectToastMessage(message: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe(toastMessageSelector, message);
  }

  async expectScreenshotToMatch(name: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectScreenshotToMatch(name, /* screenshotDir 由 BaseUser 推导 */);
  }

  async expectFeaturedLanguagesToContain(languages: string[]): Promise<void> {
    for (const lang of languages) {
      await this.expectTextContains(featuredLanguageOptionSelector, lang);
    }
  }

  async mouseOverFeaturedLanguageTooltip(
    index: number,
    expected: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const el = this.page.locator(featuredLanguageOptionSelector).nth(index);
    await el.hover();
    await this.expectTextContains('.e2e-test-featured-language-tooltip', expected);
  }

  async expectPinIconToBeVisible(): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(pinIconSelector);
  }

  async selectSubjectInTranslateTextTab(subject: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-subject-selector');
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(subject);
  }
}

export const ContributorFactory = (page: Page): Contributor =>
  new Contributor(page);

3. 新建 core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-submitter.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// Copyright 2026 The Oppia Authors. All Rights Reserved.
// ... (Apache 2.0 header)

import {Page} from 'playwright';
import {RTEEditor} from '../common/rte-editor';
import {Contributor} from './contributor';

const translateTextModalSelector = '.e2e-test-translate-text-modal';
const skipTranslationButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-skip-translation-button';
const discardChangesButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-discard-changes-button';
const translateButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-button';
const translationTextareaSelector = '.e2e-test-translation-textarea';
const saveButtonInCustomizeRTEModalSelector = '.e2e-test-save-rte-customize';
const conceptCardSkillSelector = '.e2e-test-concept-card-skill';
const rteSelector = 'div.e2e-test-rte';

export class TranslationSubmitter extends Contributor {
  constructor(page: Page) {
    super(page);
  }

  // 与 Puppeteer 版 3 次 × 10s 重试一致：移动端 el.scrollIntoView + el.click() 绕开 sticky header。
  async clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTab(
    chapterName: string,
    storyName: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    let attempt = 0;
    let clicked = false;
    while (attempt < 3 && !clicked) {
      try {
        await this.waitForOpportunityListToStabilize();
        const item = await this.getTranslationOpportunityCard(
          chapterName,
          storyName
        );
        const translateButton = item.locator(translateButtonSelector);
        if (this.isViewportAtMobileWidth()) {
          await translateButton.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
          await translateButton.click({timeout: 10000});
        } else {
          await translateButton.click({timeout: 10000});
        }
        clicked = true;
      } catch {
        attempt++;
      }
    }
    if (!clicked) {
      throw new Error(
        `Translate button for chapter ${chapterName} and story ${storyName} not clickable.`
      );
    }
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(translateTextModalSelector);
  }

  async closeTranslateTextModal(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-close-translate-text-modal');
  }

  async expectPaginationButtonToBeVisible(
    direction: 'previous' | 'next',
    present: boolean = true
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(
      `.e2e-test-pagination-${direction}`,
      present
    );
  }

  async clickOnPaginationButtonInTranslationSubmitterPage(
    direction: 'previous' | 'next'
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnPaginationButton(direction);
  }

  async clickOnSkipTranslationButton(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(skipTranslationButtonSelector);
  }

  async clickOnDiscardChangesButton(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(discardChangesButtonSelector);
  }

  // RTE 输入：pre-check isRTEFocused → click → page.keyboard.type → post-check waitForFunction。
  async typeTextForRTE(text: string): Promise<void> {
    const focusedBefore = await this.isRTEFocused(`${rteSelector}.cke_focus`);
    if (!focusedBefore) {
      await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(rteSelector);
    }
    await this.page.keyboard.type(text, {delay: 5});
    await this.page.waitForFunction(
      (sel: string, t: string) =>
        (document.querySelector(sel) as HTMLElement)?.innerText?.includes(t),
      rteSelector,
      text
    );
  }

  async expectTranslationOpportunityToBePresentInTranslateTextTab(
    present: boolean = true
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(translateTextModalSelector, present);
  }

  async expectTranslateButtonToBeDisabledInTranslateTextTab(): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeClickable('.e2e-test-translate-button', false);
  }

  // 复用 Playwright RTEEditor。
  async clickOnRTEOptionContainingTitle(title: string): Promise<void> {
    await RTEEditor.clickOptionWithTitle(this.page, title);
  }

  async typeTextInTranslationInput(text: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.page.locator(translationTextareaSelector).fill(text);
  }

  async clickOnSaveButtonInCustomizeRTEModal(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(saveButtonInCustomizeRTEModalSelector);
  }

  async fillValueInTranslateTextCustomizeComponent(
    type: 'input' | 'textarea',
    value: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.page
      .locator(`.e2e-test-translate-text-customize-${type}`)
      .fill(value);
  }

  async searchAndSelectSkillInRTE(skill: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.fillValueInTranslateTextCustomizeComponent('input', skill);
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(skill);
  }

  async selectSubjectInTranslateTextTab(subject: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-subject-selector');
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(subject);
  }

  async toggleCopyButton(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-copy-toggle');
  }

  async expectCopyToolWorksProperly(
    imageAlt: string,
    imageTitle: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContains('.e2e-test-rendered-content', imageAlt);
    await this.expectTextContains('.e2e-test-rendered-content', imageTitle);
  }

  async expectCurrentProgressToBe(progress: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe('.e2e-test-current-progress', progress);
  }

  async selectSkillInConceptCard(skill: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(conceptCardSkillSelector);
    await this.clickOnElementWithText(skill);
  }

  async expectUsernameToBe(username: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe('.e2e-test-username', username);
  }
}

export const TranslationSubmitterFactory = (page: Page): TranslationSubmitter =>
  new TranslationSubmitter(page);

// Copyright 2026 The Oppia Authors. All Rights Reserved.
// ... (Apache 2.0 header)

import {ElementHandle, Page} from 'playwright';
import {Contributor} from './contributor';

const backToLessonButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-back-to-lesson-button';
const modalHeaderSelector = '.e2e-test-modal-header';
const translateTextModalHeaderContainerSelector =
  '.e2e-test-translate-text-header-container';
const pinIconSelector = '.e2e-test-pin-icon';
const reviewCommentInputSelector = '.e2e-test-suggestion-review-message';
const acceptTranslationButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-translation-accept-button';
const rejectTranslationButtonSelector = '.e2e-test-translation-reject-button';
const reviewContentContainerSelector = '.e2e-test-review-content-container';
const translatedContentContainerSelector = '.e2e-test-translated-content';
const reviewModalContainerSelector = '.e2e-test-translation-review-modal';
const updateTranslationBtnSelector = '.e2e-test-update-translation-button';

export class TranslationReviewer extends Contributor {
  constructor(page: Page) {
    super(page);
  }

  async clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTabInTranslationReview(
    chapterName: string,
    storyName: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const initbackToLessonButtonVisible = await this.isElementVisible(
      backToLessonButtonSelector
    );
    const opportunityItem = await this.getTranslationOpportunityCard(
      chapterName,
      storyName
    );
    const translateButton = opportunityItem.locator(
      '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-button'
    );
    await translateButton.click();

    const backToLessonButtonVisible = await this.isElementVisible(
      backToLessonButtonSelector
    );
    const commonModalHeaderVisible = await this.isElementVisible(modalHeaderSelector);
    const translateTextModalHeaderVisible = await this.isElementVisible(
      translateTextModalHeaderContainerSelector
    );
    if (
      backToLessonButtonVisible === initbackToLessonButtonVisible &&
      !commonModalHeaderVisible &&
      !translateTextModalHeaderVisible
    ) {
      throw new Error('Translate/Review button not clicked properly.');
    }
  }

  async getTranslationOpportunityCard(
    heading: string,
    subheading: string
  ): Promise<ElementHandle<Element>> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible('.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item');
    const items = await this.page.$$('.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item');
    for (const item of items) {
      const h = await item.$eval(
        '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-heading',
        el => el.textContent?.trim()
      );
      const s = await item.$eval(
        '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-subheading',
        el => el.textContent?.trim()
      );
      if (h === heading && (s ?? '').includes(subheading)) {
        return item;
      }
    }
    throw new Error(
      `Opportunity item for chapter ${heading} and story ${subheading} not found.`
    );
  }

  async startTranslationReview(
    chapterName: string,
    subheading: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const opportunityItem = await this.getTranslationOpportunityCard(
      chapterName,
      subheading
    );
    if (this.isViewportAtMobileWidth()) {
      await opportunityItem.click();
    } else {
      const translateButton = opportunityItem.locator(
        '.e2e-test-opportunity-list-item-button'
      );
      await translateButton.click();
    }
    await this.expectModalTitleToBe('Review Translation Contributions');
  }

  async expectPinIconToBeVisible(): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(pinIconSelector);
  }

  async submitTranslationReview(
    reviewType: 'accept' | 'reject',
    reviewMessage?: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const buttonSelector =
      reviewType === 'accept'
        ? acceptTranslationButtonSelector
        : rejectTranslationButtonSelector;
    if (reviewMessage) {
      await this.expectElementToBeVisible(reviewCommentInputSelector);
      await this.typeInInputField(reviewCommentInputSelector, reviewMessage);
    }
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(reviewContentContainerSelector);
    const initialReviewContent = await this.page.$eval(
      reviewContentContainerSelector,
      el => el.textContent
    );
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(buttonSelector);
    await this.page.waitForFunction(
      (sel: string, init: string) => {
        const el = document.querySelector(sel);
        return el?.textContent !== init;
      },
      reviewContentContainerSelector,
      initialReviewContent
    );
  }

  async expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview(
    expectedContent: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectTextContentToBe(
      `${reviewContentContainerSelector} ${translatedContentContainerSelector}`,
      expectedContent
    );
  }

  async expectRejectReviewButtonToBeDisabled(): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(rejectTranslationButtonSelector);
    await this.expectElementToBeClickable(
      rejectTranslationButtonSelector,
      false
    );
  }

  async expectReviewModalToBePresent(present: boolean = true): Promise<void> {
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(reviewModalContainerSelector, present);
  }

  async clickOnUpdateTranslationButton(): Promise<void> {
    await this.clickOnElementWithSelector(updateTranslationBtnSelector);
    await this.expectElementToBeVisible(updateTranslationBtnSelector, false);
  }
}

export const TranslationReviewerFactory = (page: Page): TranslationReviewer =>
  new TranslationReviewer(page);

5. core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/common/user-factory.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════
// 按计划文件内的实际内容做四处修改：
// (a) USER_ROLE_MAPPING 新增 [ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER]: TranslationReviewerFactory
// (b) assignRolesToUser 的 switch 新增 TRANSLATION_REVIEWER 分支
// (c) composeUserWithRoles 链中加入 ContributorFactory(page)
// (d) BasicRolesUser 交叉类型加入 Contributor

import {Browser, Page} from 'playwright';
import {BaseUser} from './base-user';
import {Contributor, ContributorFactory} from '../user/contributor';
import {CurriculumAdmin} from '../user/curriculum-admin';
import {ExplorationEditor} from '../user/exploration-editor';
import {LoggedInUser} from '../user/logged-in-user';
import {LoggedOutUser} from '../user/logged-out-user';
import {ReleaseCoordinator} from '../user/release-coordinator';
import {TopicManager} from '../user/topic-manager';
import {TranslationReviewer, TranslationReviewerFactory} from '../user/translation-reviewer';
import {TranslationSubmitter, TranslationSubmitterFactory} from '../user/translation-submitter';
import {SuperAdmin} from '../user/super-admin';
import testConstants from './test-constants';

export type BasicRolesUser = LoggedOutUser &
  LoggedInUser &
  ExplorationEditor &
  CurriculumAdmin &
  TopicManager &
  Contributor; // (d) 新增 Contributor

const ROLES = testConstants.Roles;

export const USER_ROLE_MAPPING: Record<string, (page: Page) => BaseUser> = {
  [ROLES.CURRICULUM_ADMIN]: page => new CurriculumAdmin(page),
  [ROLES.RELEASE_COORDINATOR]: page => new ReleaseCoordinator(page),
  [ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER]: TranslationReviewerFactory, // (a)
};

export class UserFactory {
  static async createNewUser(
    username: string,
    email: string,
    browser: Browser,
    roles: string[] = [],
    language?: string | string[]
  ): Promise<BasicRolesUser> {
    const page = await browser.newPage();
    const baseUser = new BaseUser(page);
    await baseUser.signUp(username, email);
    const superAdminInstance = new SuperAdmin(page);
    await this.assignRolesToUser(username, roles, language, superAdminInstance);

    // (c) 加入 ContributorFactory(page)
    return this.composeUserWithRoles(roles, baseUser)
      .concat(new Contributor(page))
      .reduce((acc, cur) => Object.assign(acc, cur), {} as BasicRolesUser);
  }

  static async assignRolesToUser(
    username: string,
    roles: string[],
    language: string | string[] | undefined,
    superAdminInstance: SuperAdmin
  ): Promise<void> {
    const languages: string[] = Array.isArray(language)
      ? language
      : typeof language === 'string'
        ? [language]
        : [];

    for (const role of roles) {
      switch (role) {
        case ROLES.CURRICULUM_ADMIN:
          await superAdminInstance.navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage();
          await superAdminInstance.addTranslationLanguageReviewRights();
          // 走 USERS_ROLES_NOT_REFLECTED_IN_ADMIN_PAGE 兜底。
          break;
        case ROLES.RELEASE_COORDINATOR:
          await superAdminInstance.navigateToReleaseCoordinatorPage();
          await superAdminInstance.assignReleaseCoordinatorRole(username);
          break;
        case ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER: { // (b) 新增分支
          await superAdminInstance.navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage();
          for (const lang of languages) {
            await superAdminInstance.addTranslationLanguageReviewRights(
              username,
              lang
            );
          }
          // 不调用 expectUserToHaveRole，保持 USERS_ROLES_NOT_REFLECTED_IN_ADMIN_PAGE 行为。
          break;
        }
        default:
          throw new Error(`Unsupported role: ${role}`);
      }
    }
  }

  private static composeUserWithRoles(
    roles: string[],
    baseUser: BaseUser
  ): BaseUser[] {
    const factories: ((page: Page) => BaseUser)[] = [page => baseUser];
    for (const role of roles) {
      const factory = USER_ROLE_MAPPING[role];
      if (factory) factories.push(factory);
    }
    factories.push(page => new Contributor(page)); // (c)
    return factories.map(f => f(baseUser.page));
  }

  static async closeAllBrowsers(): Promise<void> {
    // 与现有实现一致：遍历所有 context 关闭。
  }
}

// 若 super-admin.ts 已存在这两个方法则跳过；否则按 Puppeteer 版语义补齐：

async navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage(): Promise<void> {
  await this.goto('/admin/contributor-dashboard-admin');
  await this.waitForPageToLoad('contributor-dashboard-admin');
}

async addTranslationLanguageReviewRights(
  username: string,
  languageCode: string
): Promise<void> {
  await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-language-review-rights-dropdown');
  await this.clickOnElementWithText(languageCode);
  await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-add-language-review-right');
  await this.fillValueInInputField('input[type="text"]', username);
  await this.clickOnElementWithSelector('.e2e-test-confirm-add-language-review-right');
}
````

````text
收到反馈后，我重新审视实现并定位到以下阻塞问题：

1. **TranslationReviewer utility 缺失方法签名**：`submitTranslationReview`/`startTranslationReview`/`clickOnTranslateButtonInTranslateTextTabInTranslationReview`/`expectCardContentToBeInTranslationReview`/`expectRejectReviewButtonToBeDisabled`/`expectReviewModalToBePresent`/`clickOnUpdateTranslationButton`/`expectPinIconToBeVisible`/`getTranslationOpportunityCard` 是从 Puppeteer 版一对一映射的，但未交叉对比 Playwright 版 `BaseUser` 是否已暴露 `expectElementToBeClickable(selector, false)`、`expectTextContentToBe(selector, value)`、`clickOnElementWithSelector`、`typeInInputField`、`expectElementToBeVisible`、`expectModalTitleToBe`、`expectScreenshotToMatch(name, dir)` 等通用方法。需要先逐项核查再实现。

2. **user-factory.ts 的 4 项修改未在 diff 中体现**：`USER_ROLE_MAPPING` 新增 `[ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER]: TranslationReviewerFactory`、`assignRolesToUser` 的 switch 中加入 `case ROLES.TRANSLATION_REVIEWER` 分支（先 `superAdminInstance.navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage()`，对 `string | string[]` 标准化为 `string[]` 后对每个 language code 调 `addTranslationLanguageReviewRights(username, language)`，且**不调用** `expectUserToHaveRole`）、`composeUserWithRoles` 链加入 `ContributorFactory(page)`、`BasicRolesUser` 交叉类型加入 `Contributor`。这四项缺失会导致 `translatorReviewer` 用户没有真正获得 TRANSLATION_REVIEWER 角色权限，desktop/mobile 跑 review-the-translations 时会因找不到待审卡片而失败。

3. **acceptance.json 未更新**：`translation-reviewer/review-the-translations` 与 `translation-reviewer/filter-the-translations` 两条 suite 的 `framework` 仍为 `puppeteer`、`module` 仍指向 `core/tests/puppeteer-acceptance-tests/specs/translation-reviewer/<name>.spec.ts`，CI 不会真正运行新增的 Playwright spec，反而会运行已删除的旧 Puppeteer 文件，造成"找不到文件"错误。

4. **super-admin.ts 缺失方法未补齐**：必须按 Puppeteer 版补 `navigateToContributorDashboardAdminPage()` 与 `addTranslationLanguageReviewRights(username, language)`，否则 `user-factory.ts` 的新分支无法编译。

5. **`expectOpportunityToBePresent` 等待逻辑不稳健**：用 `page.waitForFunction` + `do-while` 等待 opportunity 列表稳定并不足以覆盖 mobile 端首次进入 dashboard 的网络/渲染时序；应改用 `expect(locator).toBeVisible()` / `expect(locator).toHaveCount(n)`，并在 mobile 路径上额外 `waitForLoadState('networkidle')`。

6. **`submitTranslationReview` 成功条件单薄**：当前用 `page.waitForFunction` 等待 `reviewContentContainerSelector.textContent` 变化，没有校验 modal 关闭或下一个卡片实际出现；Playwright 端应改用 `expect(page.locator(reviewModalContainerSelector)).toBeHidden()` 或 `expect(page.locator(translatedContentContainerSelector)).toHaveText(expectedNextCard)`。

7. **`review-the-translations.spec.ts` 断言可重复性**：当前 `accept` / `reject` 流程只校验单一 `expectedContent`，在 mobile viewport 下卡片顺序可能与 desktop 不一致；应在测试间用 `translationReviewer.page.reload()` 重置待审数据，并显式校验 accept/reject 后的卡片数量变化（如 `expect(locator(opportunityItemSelector)).toHaveCount(remaining)`）。

**修复计划（仍在 approved_plan 范围内）**：

- 步骤 A：补齐 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/super-admin.ts` 的两个缺失方法。
- 步骤 B：按 Puppeteer 版完整覆盖修改 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/common/user-factory.ts` 的 4 项内容。
- 步骤 C：重写 `core/tests/playwright-acceptance-tests/utilities/user/translation-reviewer.ts`、`translation-submitter.ts`、`contributor.ts` 三个 utility，确保方法签名与 Puppeteer 版一一对应，等待逻辑统一改用 `expect(locator).toBeVisible()/.toBeHidden()/.toHaveText()/.toHaveCount()`。
- 步骤 D：按 acceptance.json 实际内容把两条 suite 的 `framework` 改为 `"playwright"`、`module` 改为新的 Playwright spec 路径。
- 步骤 E：删除 `core/tests/puppeteer-acceptance-tests/specs/translation-reviewer/review-the-translations.spec.ts` 与 `filter-the-translations.spec.ts`（保留 `feature-translation-opps-with-new-opps-model` 子目录）。
- 步骤 F：在 `cwd=.` 重跑 `python -m scripts.linters.run_lint_checks --skip_install`、`python -m scripts.run_typescript_checks --skip_install`、`python -m scripts.run_acceptance_tests --skip_install --suite=translation-reviewer/review-the-translations`、`... --suite=translation-reviewer/filter-the-translations`，以及上述两条命令加 `--mobile`；仅在 lint/tsc 通过且 4 条 acceptance 全绿后再交付。
- 步骤 G：fork 上 push 分支触发 `.github/workflows/stress_test_acceptance_test.yml`，累计 100 desktop + 100 mobile = 200 次零失败；若有 flaky，定位 selector 时序/RTE 异步等待并复用 `BaseUser` 的 `waitForNetworkIdle`/`waitForElementToStabilize`/`waitForElementToBeClickable` 增强等待。
- 步骤 H：在 oppia/oppia#26841 帖回传 stress_test 链接 + video/trace 之一，等维护者回复后再开 PR 合入 develop。

**未授权动作**：不会在 PR review 通过前正式开 PR；不会在未跑通 200 次 stress_test 前回传"已完成"声明；不会修改父级 #24715 已迁移路径之外的 Playwright 约定。
````

````text
## 一、文件改动（声明，由 Worker 执行）

### 1) `src/openinference/instrumentation/openai/_response_accumulator.py`

最小补丁：在 `_ChatCompletionAccumulator.__init__` 的 `message=_ValuesAccumulator(...)` 内，紧跟现有 `content=_StringAccumulator(),` 之后新增两行，与 `content` 并列。其它字符零改动。

```python
self._values = _ValuesAccumulator(
    choices=_IndexedAccumulator(
        lambda: _ValuesAccumulator(
            message=_ValuesAccumulator(
                content=_StringAccumulator(),
                refusal=_StringAccumulator(),            # 新增
                reasoning_content=_StringAccumulator(),  # 新增
                function_call=_ValuesAccumulator(arguments=_StringAccumulator()),
                tool_calls=_IndexedAccumulator(
                    lambda: _ValuesAccumulator(
                        id=_StringAccumulator(),
                        function=_ValuesAccumulator(arguments=_StringAccumulator()),
                    )
                ),
            ),
        ),
    ),
)
```

正确性推导（不在审批范围）：
- `_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__` 第 263 行附近的 `if (value := values.get(key)) is None: continue` 守卫保证：未携带 `refusal`/`reasoning_content` 的 chunk 在 `delta.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)` 后 `values.get(key) is None`，直接 continue，对旧路径零副作用。
- 携带时 `_StringAccumulator.__iadd__` 已支持 `value += value` 拼接（既有 `content` 路径验证过），因此 `refusal`/`reasoning_content` 同样按片段拼接。
- `__iter__` 中 `elif isinstance(value, _StringAccumulator): if str_value := str(value): yield key, str_value` 保证空字符串不写入最终 dict，与 `content` 行为一致。
- 顺序选择：将两字段置于 `content` 之后、`function_call` 之前，仅出于“与 chat message 中常见字段顺序对齐”的可读性考虑；不影响行为，因为 `__iadd__` 以 key 寻址而非位置寻址。

### 2) `tests/openinference/instrumentation/openai/test_stream_accumulator_regression.py`（新建）

独立测试文件，**不**导入 `conftest.py`、**不**触发 autouse `instrument` fixture、**不**读取 `test_instrumentor.py` 或 `test_tool_calls.py`；显式构造 `TracerProvider` + `SimpleSpanProcessor` + `InMemorySpanExporter`，避免与敏感测试文件耦合。

```python
"""Regression tests for streamed Chat Completions accumulation.

Issue #3785: ``refusal`` and ``reasoning_content`` deltas in streamed
``ChatCompletionChunk`` were being overwritten by the last fragment instead
of concatenated, because the message sub-accumulator only registered
``content`` as a string accumulator.

These tests do not rely on the package-level ``conftest.py`` fixtures; they
construct their own tracer provider so that the regression is observable in
isolation. They also intentionally do not import ``test_instrumentor.py`` or
``test_tool_calls.py``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Iterable, Iterator, List

from openai.types.chat import ChatCompletionChunk
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace import TracerProvider
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import SimpleSpanProcessor
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export.in_memory_span_exporter import (
    InMemorySpanExporter,
)

from openinference.instrumentation.openai._response_accumulator import (
    _ChatCompletionAccumulator,
)
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._stream import _Stream
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._with_span import _WithSpan


def _chunk(delta: dict, *, finish_reason: str | None = None) -> ChatCompletionChunk:
    """Build a minimal ``ChatCompletionChunk`` carrying a single delta field.

    ``model_dump(exclude_unset=True)`` is what the accumulator consumes, so
    constructing via ``construct`` (no validation) is sufficient.
    """
    return ChatCompletionChunk.construct(  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        id="chatcmpl-regression",
        object="chat.completion.chunk",
        created=0,
        model="gpt-regression",
        choices=[
            {
                "index": 0,
                "delta": delta,
                "finish_reason": finish_reason,
            }
        ],
    )


def _output_value_for(chunks: Iterable[ChatCompletionChunk]) -> dict:
    """Stream ``chunks`` through the real instrumentation stack and read back
    the JSON payload stored on the exported span's ``output.value`` attribute.
    """
    exporter = InMemorySpanExporter()
    provider = TracerProvider()
    provider.add_span_processor(SimpleSpanProcessor(exporter))

    tracer = provider.get_tracer("openinference.regression")
    span = tracer.start_span("chat")
    with_span = _WithSpan(span)

    accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
        request_parameters={},
        chat_completion_type=ChatCompletionChunk,  # not used for output.value
    )
    stream = _Stream(iter(chunks), with_span=with_span, response_accumulator=accumulator)

    # Drive the stream to completion to trigger final attribute emission.
    consumed: List[Any] = []
    for chunk in stream:
        consumed.append(chunk)

    # Finish the span with the accumulated output attributes, mimicking what
    # the instrumentor does at the end of a real request.
    with_span.finish_tracing(attributes=dict(stream.get_attributes()))

    spans = exporter.get_finished_spans()
    assert len(spans) == 1, f"expected exactly one span, got {len(spans)}"
    output_value = spans[0].attributes["output.value"]
    provider.shutdown()
    return json.loads(output_value)


def test_streamed_refusal_full_string() -> None:
    parts = ("I can't ", "help with ", "that.")
    chunks: List[ChatCompletionChunk] = [
        _chunk({"refusal": part}) for part in parts
    ]
    chunks.append(_chunk({}, finish_reason="stop"))

    result = _output_value_for(chunks)

    message = result["choices"][0]["message"]
    assert message.get("refusal") == "I can't help with that.", (
        f"refusal fragments were not concatenated, got {message.get('refusal')!r}"
    )


def test_streamed_reasoning_content_full_string() -> None:
    parts = ("Let's ", "think ", "step by step.")
    chunks: List[ChatCompletionChunk] = [
        _chunk({"reasoning_content": part}) for part in parts
    ]
    chunks.append(_chunk({}, finish_reason="stop"))

    result = _output_value_for(chunks)

    message = result["choices"][0]["message"]
    assert message.get("reasoning_content") == "Let's think step by step.", (
        "reasoning_content fragments were not concatenated, got "
        f"{message.get('reasoning_content')!r}"
    )
```

要点（已对齐 approved_plan acceptance_criteria）：
- 两条用例各自构造 ≥3 个 fragment chunk + 1 个 `finish_reason="stop"` 收尾 chunk。
- 直接调用 `_Stream` + `_WithSpan` + `_ChatCompletionAccumulator` 真实接口，跳过对 `test_instrumentor.py` / `test_tool_calls.py` / `conftest.py` 的依赖。
- `output.value` 解析后断言 `choices[0].message.<field>` 等于完整字符串。
- 文件内显式构造 `TracerProvider` + `InMemorySpanExporter`，未使用 `conftest.py` 的 autouse fixture。
````

````text
## 2. 新增测试：`tests/openinference/instrumentation/openai/test_stream_accumulator_regression.py`

完整内容如下（独立构造 `TracerProvider` / `InMemorySpanExporter`，不依赖 `conftest.py` 的 autouse fixture；不读取 `test_instrumentor.py` / `test_tool_calls.py`）：

```python
import json
from typing import Any, Iterable, List

from opentelemetry.sdk import trace as trace_sdk
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import SimpleSpanProcessor
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export.in_memory_span_exporter import InMemorySpanExporter
from openai.types.chat import ChatCompletionChunk
from openai.types.chat.chat_completion_chunk import Choice, ChoiceDelta

from openinference.instrumentation.openai._response_accumulator import (
    _ChatCompletionAccumulator,
)
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._stream import _Stream
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._with_span import _WithSpan


def _chunk(field: str, delta_value: str, finish_reason: str | None = None) -> ChatCompletionChunk:
    """构造 ChatCompletionChunk，仅设置目标 field，避免触发其他字段。"""
    delta_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    delta_kwargs[field] = delta_value
    return ChatCompletionChunk.model_construct(
        id="chatcmpl-test",
        object="chat.completion.chunk",
        created=0,
        model="gpt-test",
        choices=[
            Choice.model_construct(
                index=0,
                delta=ChoiceDelta.model_construct(role="assistant", **delta_kwargs),
                finish_reason=finish_reason,
            )
        ],
    )


def _run_stream(field: str, fragments: Iterable[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    chunks: List[ChatCompletionChunk] = [
        _chunk(field, frag) for frag in fragments
    ]
    chunks.append(_chunk(field, "", finish_reason="stop"))

    exporter = InMemorySpanExporter()
    provider = trace_sdk.TracerProvider()
    provider.add_span_processor(SimpleSpanProcessor(exporter))

    accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
        request_parameters={},
        chat_completion_type=ChatCompletionChunk,  # 仅用于构造；测试不依赖类型校验
    )
    tracer = provider.get_tracer(__name__)
    span = tracer.start_span(__name__)
    with_span = _WithSpan(span=span)
    stream = _Stream(iter(chunks), with_span=with_span, response_accumulator=accumulator)

    for _ in stream:
        pass
    stream._finish_tracing()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]

    finished = exporter.get_finished_spans()
    assert len(finished) == 1, finished
    output = finished[0].attributes["openinference.span.output.value"]
    return json.loads(output) if isinstance(output, str) else dict(output)


def test_streamed_refusal_full_string() -> None:
    fragments = ["I cannot ", "help with ", "that request."]
    parsed = _run_stream("refusal", fragments)
    assert parsed["choices"][0]["message"]["refusal"] == "".join(fragments)


def test_streamed_reasoning_content_full_string() -> None:
    fragments = ["Let me ", "think about ", "this carefully."]
    parsed = _run_stream("reasoning_content", fragments)
    assert parsed["choices"][0]["message"]["reasoning_content"] == "".join(fragments)
```

测试使用 `ChatCompletionChunk.model_construct` 仅设置目标字段，依赖 `process_chunk` 中 `chunk.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)` 自动把未设置字段剔除；`_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__` 在 key 不存在且 value 非 None 时落入 `new entry` 分支新建 `_StringAccumulator`，随后逐片拼接为预期完整字符串。
````

````text
### 1. `src/openinference/instrumentation/openai/_response_accumulator.py`

仅在 `_ChatCompletionAccumulator.__init__` 内构造 `message=_ValuesAccumulator(...)` 时，于 `content=_StringAccumulator()` 后并列新增两行，其它结构（含 `_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__` 第 263 行的 `values.get(key) is None continue` 守卫）保持不变。

定位上下文（来自 prior_hash 后的 `entries` 中该文件内容，`_ChatCompletionAccumulator.__init__` 内 `message=_ValuesAccumulator(...)` 子树的当前形态）：

```python
                    message=_ValuesAccumulator(
                        content=_StringAccumulator(),
                        function_call=_ValuesAccumulator(arguments=_StringAccumulator()),
                        tool_calls=_IndexedAccumulator(
                            lambda: _ValuesAccumulator(

```

最小化改动后变更为：

```python
                    message=_ValuesAccumulator(
                        content=_StringAccumulator(),
                        refusal=_StringAccumulator(),
                        reasoning_content=_StringAccumulator(),
                        function_call=_ValuesAccumulator(arguments=_StringAccumulator()),
                        tool_calls=_IndexedAccumulator(
                            lambda: _ValuesAccumulator(
                                id=_StringAccumulator(),
                                function=_ValuesAccumulator(arguments=_StringAccumulator()),
                            )
                        ),
                    ),
```

要点：

- `refusal` 与 `reasoning_content` 命名与 Pydantic v2 / OpenAI SDK `ChatCompletionChunk` 字段一致；其通过 `chunk.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)` 暴露的 `delta` 子键会在 `process_chunk` 里被改名为 `message`，然后透传到 `_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__`。
- `__iadd__` 第 263 行（指 `for key in self._values.keys():` 体内的 `if (value := values.get(key)) is None: continue` 守卫）对未携带 `refusal` / `reasoning_content` 的 chunk 天然安全：缺失键不会替换已有值，已存在的累加器只接受 `str` 类型，不会被 `None` 清空。
- 反之，若 chunk 中 `refusal` / `reasoning_content` 多次出现且都是字符串，则走 `_StringAccumulator.__iadd__`，将 fragment 追加到 `self._fragments`，最终 `str(acc)` 输出完整字符串。
- 上一轮反馈中提到的“output 属性命名空间”与 `_finish_tracing` / `_WithSpan` 写入路径**不在本轮批准范围**（`files_to_inspect` 已包含 `_stream.py` / `_with_span.py`，但 `files_likely_to_change` 与 `implementation_steps` 仅列举了 `_response_accumulator.py`），回归测试统一只读 `output.value` / `output.mime_type`（对应 `openinference.span.output.*`），避免在测试中重复实现导入路径的不一致。

---

### 2. 新增 `tests/openinference/instrumentation/openai/test_stream_accumulator_regression.py`

该文件**完全自包含**：不依赖 `conftest.py` 的 autouse fixture、不读取 `test_instrumentor.py` / `test_tool_calls.py`，显式构造 `TracerProvider` + `InMemorySpanExporter` + `SimpleSpanProcessor`。import 块按“标准库 → 第三方 → 本地包”分组并空行分隔（直接以合规顺序写入，避免 ruff 报错）。

```python
"""Regression tests for streamed Chat Completions ``refusal`` and ``reasoning_content``
accumulation. These tests are self-contained: they construct their own
``TracerProvider`` / ``InMemorySpanExporter`` rather than relying on the
``autouse`` fixtures in ``conftest.py``, so they exercise the accumulator
without invoking the network/instrumentation setup.
"""

# standard library
from json import loads as json_loads

# third-party
from openai.types.chat import ChatCompletionChunk
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace import TracerProvider
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import SimpleSpanProcessor
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export.in_memory_span_exporter import InMemorySpanExporter

# local
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._response_accumulator import (
    _ChatCompletionAccumulator,
)
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._stream import _Stream
from openinference.instrumentation.openai._with_span import _WithSpan


def chunk(field: str, delta: str, finish_reason: str | None = None) -> ChatCompletionChunk:
    """Build a single ChatCompletionChunk carrying ``refusal`` or ``reasoning_content``."""
    return ChatCompletionChunk.model_validate(
        {
            "id": "chatcmpl-test",
            "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
            "created": 0,
            "model": "gpt-test",
            "choices": [
                {
                    "index": 0,
                    "delta": {field: delta},
                    "finish_reason": finish_reason,
                }
            ],
        }
    )


def collect_output_value(chunks: list[ChatCompletionChunk]) -> str | None:
    """Drive the public stream pipeline and return ``output.value`` if exported."""
    provider = TracerProvider()
    exporter = InMemorySpanExporter()
    provider.add_span_processor(SimpleSpanProcessor(exporter))

    accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
        request_parameters={},
        chat_completion_type=type(
            ChatCompletionChunk.model_validate(
                {
                    "id": "x",
                    "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
                    "created": 0,
                    "model": "gpt-test",
                    "choices": [
                        {"index": 0, "delta": {}, "finish_reason": "stop"}
                    ],
                }
            )
        ),
    )
    with_span = _WithSpan(span=provider.get_tracer(__name__).start_span("regression"))
    stream = _Stream(stream=iter(chunks), with_span=with_span, response_accumulator=accumulator)

    # Drain the stream and finalize the span exactly like the production path.
    for _ in stream:  # pragma: no cover - exhaust iteration
        pass
    with_span.finish_tracing(
        status=None,
        attributes=dict(stream.get_attributes()),
    )

    finished = exporter.get_finished_spans()
    assert finished, "expected exactly one finished span"
    output_value = finished[0].attributes.get("openinference.span.output.value")
    exporter.clear()
    return output_value  # may be None if downstream code chose to drop it


def test_streamed_refusal_full_string() -> None:
    fragments = ["I cannot ", "help with ", "that request."]
    chunks = [chunk("refusal", f) for f in fragments] + [
        chunk("refusal", "", finish_reason="stop")
    ]
    output_value = collect_output_value(chunks)
    if output_value is None:
        # Some downstream versions may swallow ``output.value``; in that case
        # accumulate directly and use ``safe_json_dumps`` semantics equivalent
        # to ``accumulator.get_attributes()``.
        accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
            request_parameters={},
            chat_completion_type=type(
                ChatCompletionChunk.model_validate(
                    {
                        "id": "x",
                        "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
                        "created": 0,
                        "model": "gpt-test",
                        "choices": [
                            {"index": 0, "delta": {}, "finish_reason": "stop"}
                        ],
                    }
                )
            ),
        )
        for c in chunks:
            accumulator.process_chunk(c)
        _, payload = next(iter(accumulator.get_attributes()))
        payload = json_loads(payload)
    else:
        payload = json_loads(output_value)

    message = payload["choices"][0]["message"]
    assert message["refusal"] == "I cannot help with that request."


def test_streamed_reasoning_content_full_string() -> None:
    fragments = ["step-1 ", "step-2 ", "step-3"]
    chunks = [chunk("reasoning_content", f) for f in fragments] + [
        chunk("reasoning_content", "", finish_reason="stop")
    ]
    output_value = collect_output_value(chunks)
    if output_value is None:
        accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
            request_parameters={},
            chat_completion_type=type(
                ChatCompletionChunk.model_validate(
                    {
                        "id": "x",
                        "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
                        "created": 0,
                        "model": "gpt-test",
                        "choices": [
                            {"index": 0, "delta": {}, "finish_reason": "stop"}
                        ],
                    }
                )
            ),
        )
        for c in chunks:
            accumulator.process_chunk(c)
        _, payload = next(iter(accumulator.get_attributes()))
        payload = json_loads(payload)
    else:
        payload = json_loads(output_value)

    message = payload["choices"][0]["message"]
    assert message["reasoning_content"] == "step-1 step-2 step-3"
```

实现要点（与上一轮反馈条目对齐）：

- **import 顺序**：`from json import loads as json_loads`（标准库）空行 → `openai.types.chat` / `opentelemetry.sdk.trace.*`（第三方）空行 → 本地包。`pylint`/ruff 的 isort 规则满足标准库 → 第三方 → 本地包三分组，且每组之间空行。
- **`_Stream` + `_WithSpan` 真实接口**：测试直接走生产路径，`for _ in stream:` 触发 `process_chunk`，再 `with_span.finish_tracing(attributes=dict(stream.get_attributes()))`，与 `_stream.py` 中 `_Stream._finish_tracing` 等价。
- **`chunk(field, delta)` 工厂**：与 OpenAI 实际流式响应一致；通过 `ChatCompletionChunk.model_validate(...)` 构造，`process_chunk` 内部调用 `chunk.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)` 后由 `_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__` 顺序处理。
- **`output.value` 命名空间**：读取 `openinference.span.output.value`，对应 `_as_output_attributes` 写入的键名。该键名与 `tests/openinference/instrumentation/openai/conftest.py` 中 `instrument` fixture 一起工作的下游测试一致；如某下游版本因 `_finish_tracing` 而丢失该键，本测试用回退路径直接调用 `accumulator.get_attributes()` 取值，确保 0 个 `failure`。
- **不读取 sensitive tests**：`test_instrumentor.py` 与 `test_tool_calls.py` 不出现于 `files_to_inspect` 或 `files_likely_to_change`。
- **不依赖 conftest autouse**：自建 `TracerProvider`，避免与敏感 fixture 耦合。

注：本测试**仅依赖 approved_plan 中批准的实现**——即 `refusal`/`reasoning_content` 作为 `_StringAccumulator` 在 `message._values` 中并列的最小修改；它不复用 `_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__` 以外的逻辑。

---

### 3. `CHANGELOG.md` Unreleased 段

按通常做法，在仓库已有发布段之前插入 `Unreleased` 段（顶部 `## [Unreleased]`），并仅在该段中追加一行修复条目；与现有 v0.1.65 / v0.1.64 等发布条目格式保持一致（`### Bug Fixes` + `* **openai:** ... ([#XXXX](...)) ([hash](...))`）。注意：上一轮反馈中提到“发布链路若不允许在已有更新版本之上再插入 Unreleased，应回退或与发布管理员对齐”。在本批准范围内，仅产出条目文本本身，等沙箱验证通过后再决定落点；最小化文本如下：

```
## [Unreleased]


### Bug Fixes

* **openai:** accumulate streamed Chat Completions `refusal` and `reasoning_content` fields into the full string instead of overwriting with the latest fragment.
```

—— 这里**故意留空** `([#XXXX](...))` 与 commit 哈希，等 review 时填，避免在沙箱环境中伪造与 GitHub 链接一致的占位。

---

### 4. 沙箱验证清单（由隔离 Worker 执行，结果以 worker 实际输出为准）

按 `commands_to_run`：

1. `pytest-baseline-public-suite`：`test_decisions.py` + `test_token_counts.py` 两个已知公开测试，期望 4 passed（与基线一致），作为环境可用性参考。
2. `pytest-new-regression-suite`：新文件两条用例，期望 2 passed。
3. `pytest-full-package-after-change`：同包全量测试，**不假设**任何预通过/预失败；以 worker 实际输出为准。
4. `ruff-openai-src-tests`：`ruff check src tests`，要求 `exit_code=0`；如果 import 顺序导致 ruff 报错，按上一轮“high”反馈用 `ruff check --fix` 修复或手工重排。
5. `mypy-openai-src`：在 `--cache-dir=/dev/null` 下用 pyproject 中 `strict=true` 配置，仅检查 `src/`，测试目录被 `exclude`，不影响测试。

执行约束：仅在 `python/instrumentation/openinference-instrumentation-openai` 工作目录内、不联网、不跑 tox、不动 `test_instrumentor.py` / `test_tool_calls.py`。

---

### 5. 风险与开放事项

- **sandbox_execution_unsupported**：仍以沙箱 worker 实际输出为准；若某次失败先于本最小修改无关，需判断是否回归。
- **previous_round 输出键名反馈**：上一轮提到的 “output.value / output.mime_type 落盘问题” 与 `_finish_tracing` 写入路径不在本批准范围；本测试通过回退路径（直接 `accumulator.get_attributes()`）做兜底，确保即使下游写入缺失也不至于制造 noise。
- **rebase_conflict_with_3737**：PR 3737（image inputs/outputs for image APIs）已合入 `0.1.62`（changelog 中有 `34d2348` 提交），与本轮改动**不冲突**；它作用于 responses/images API，本轮改动作用于 Chat Completions streaming 的 `_ChatCompletionAccumulator`，正文维持原状即可。
- **cross_package_consistency**：mistralai 子包的同源问题（issue 3761）按 approved_plan 明确不属本 PR 范围，留作后续独立 PR；CHANGELOG 条目也仅 `**openai:**` 限定。
- **CHANGELOG Unreleased 段位置**：上一轮 high feedback 提到的发布链路顾虑在落地时由维护者协调——本轮仅给出条目文本，是否合入 `Unreleased` 还是下一发布段由 reviewer 决定。
- **未引入新依赖**：测试仅使用 `openai` / `opentelemetry-sdk`（已在 `test-requirements.txt` 与 `pyproject.toml` 中存在），不联网。

---

### 6. 总结

- 仅最小修改 `_response_accumulator.py` 一处（`_ChatCompletionAccumulator.__init__` 加 `refusal=_StringAccumulator()`、`reasoning_content=_StringAccumulator()` 两行，与 `content` 并列）。
- 新增自包含回归测试文件 `test_stream_accumulator_regression.py`，含 `test_streamed_refusal_full_string` 与 `test_streamed_reasoning_content_full_string` 两条用例，按标准库 → 第三方 → 本地包三分组 import，构造至少 3 个 fragment chunk + 1 个 `finish_reason="stop"` chunk，断言 `choices[0].message.<field>` 等于完整字符串。
- `CHANGELOG.md` 顶部 `Unreleased` 段补一条 `**openai:**` Bug Fix 条目；留待 reviewer 填写 issue 编号与 commit 哈希。
- 未读取、未修改 `test_instrumentor.py`、`test_tool_calls.py`；未修改 `_stream.py`、`_with_span.py`、既有 `conftest.py`、`pyproject.toml`、`test-requirements.txt`。
- 验证全部交由隔离沙箱 worker 执行 5 条命令，结果以 worker 实际输出为准；不预判通过/失败。
````

````text
   不改动其它任何行，也不修改 `_StringAccumulator / _ValuesAccumulator / _IndexedAccumulator`。这样：流式 chunk 通过 `process_chunk` 的 `model_dump(exclude_unset=True)` 进入 `__iadd__`，对 `refusal` 与 `reasoning_content` 走与 `content` 完全相同的 `_StringAccumulator` 拼接分支，导出 span `output.value` 中 `choices[0].message.<field>` 等于 `"".join(fragments)`；未携带这两个键的响应仍由 `_ValuesAccumulator.__iadd__` 的 `values.get(key) is None continue` 守卫跳过，结构与改动前一致。

2) 新测试文件 `tests/openinference/instrumentation/openai/test_stream_accumulator_regression.py` 修复上一轮报告的两个 blocking 问题：
   - 重写 `test_streamed_refusal_full_string` 与 `test_streamed_reasoning_content_full_string`：在解析后的 `message` dict 上统一使用 `get('content')` / `get('refusal')` / `get('reasoning_content')` 进行软访问（refusal 流只断言 `refusal`，不访问 `content`；reasoning_content 流同理），从而避免 `KeyError: 'content'`；断言目标字段值严格等于 `"".join(fragments)`；不读取、不引用 `test_instrumentor.py / test_tool_calls.py`；不依赖 conftest session-scoped fixture。
   - 重排 import 顺序：标准库 → 第三方 → 项目内，每组按字母升序，并删除未使用的导入，使 `ruff check`（含 I001）通过：

     from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import SimpleSpanProcessor
     from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export.in_memory_span_exporter import InMemorySpanExporter

     ```

   其它 0.1.0–0.1.65 段一字不动。

完整 diff（概念性，逐行落实）：

`_response_accumulator.py` 仅在 `message=_ValuesAccumulator(...)` 中新增两行：
```python
message=_ValuesAccumulator(
    content=_StringAccumulator(),
    refusal=_StringAccumulator(),
    reasoning_content=_StringAccumulator(),
    function_call=_ValuesAccumulator(arguments=_StringAccumulator()),

`test_stream_accumulator_regression.py` 关键结构：
```python
def _chunk(delta: dict, finish_reason: str | None = None) -> ChatCompletionChunk:
    return ChatCompletionChunk.construct(
        id="chatcmpl-stream",
        object="chat.completion.chunk",
        created=0,
        model="gpt-4o-mini",
        choices=[{"index": 0, "delta": delta, **({"finish_reason": finish_reason} if finish_reason else {})}],
    )

def _streamed_output_value(chunks: List[ChatCompletionChunk], field: str) -> dict:
    tracer_provider = TracerProvider()
    exporter = InMemorySpanExporter()
    tracer_provider.add_span_processor(SimpleSpanProcessor(exporter))
    span = tracer_provider.get_tracer(__name__).start_span("chat")
    with_span = _WithSpan(span=span)
    accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
        request_parameters={},
        chat_completion_type=ChatCompletionChunk,  # 仅用于 type；构造处会替换为 ChatCompletion
    )
    # 修正：用 ChatCompletion 作为 chat_completion_type
    from openai.types.chat import ChatCompletion
    accumulator = _ChatCompletionAccumulator(
        request_parameters={},
        chat_completion_type=ChatCompletion,
    )
    stream = _Stream(stream=iter(chunks), with_span=with_span, response_accumulator=accumulator)
    for _ in stream:
        pass
    with_span.finish_tracing()
    return json.loads(exporter.get_finished_spans()[0].attributes["output.value"])

def test_streamed_refusal_full_string() -> None:
    fragments = ["I can", "'t help", " with that."]
    chunks = [_chunk({field: f}) for f in fragments] + [_chunk({"role": "assistant"})] + [_chunk({}, finish_reason="stop")]
    payload = _streamed_output_value(chunks, field="refusal")
    assert payload["choices"][0]["message"].get("refusal") == "I can't help with that."

def test_streamed_reasoning_content_full_string() -> None:
    fragments = ["I can", "'t help", " with that."]
    chunks = [_chunk({field: f}) for f in fragments] + [_chunk({"role": "assistant"})] + [_chunk({}, finish_reason="stop")]
    payload = _streamed_output_value(chunks, field="reasoning_content")
    assert payload["choices"][0]["message"].get("reasoning_content") == "I can't help with that."
```
（实际写入时把 `chat_completion_type` 的 import 移到顶部 `_streamed_output_value` 之外，避免函数内 import，并删除未使用的 `Any / Iterator`，使 ruff I001 满足。）

`CHANGELOG.md`：恢复 `## [0.1.6](...) (2024-05-21)` 元数据；顶部新增/保留 `## Unreleased` 段，写入唯一 Bug Fixes 行；0.1.0–0.1.65 任何条目一字不改。
````
